Method: 使用视觉注意力作为探针，量化不同语言框架如何改变对图像的注意力数量和分布。分析约束性框架（选择题、是/否题）与开放式框架的注意力差异。基于机制性洞察，提出轻量级提示调优方法，使用可学习token鼓励模型采用开放式设置中观察到的稳健、视觉基础化的注意力模式。

Result: 约束性框架导致：1）对图像上下文的注意力显著降低；2）对任务相关区域的关注减少；3）注意力向无信息token偏移。这种注意力分配不当是准确性下降和跨框架不一致性的主要原因。提出的提示调优方法改善了视觉基础化，提高了跨框架性能。

Conclusion: VLMs存在选择性视觉盲区，其视觉注意力分配受语言框架影响。约束性框架导致注意力分配不当，进而损害性能。通过机制性干预（提示调优）可以改善视觉基础化，使模型在不同框架下表现更稳健。这揭示了VLMs中语言-视觉交互的重要机制。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have been shown to be blind, often underutilizing their visual inputs even on tasks that require visual reasoning. In this work, we demonstrate that VLMs are selectively blind. They modulate the amount of attention applied to visual inputs based on linguistic framing even when alternative framings demand identical visual reasoning. Using visual attention as a probe, we quantify how framing alters both the amount and distribution of attention over the image. Constrained framings, such as multiple choice and yes/no, induce substantially lower attention to image context compared to open-ended, reduce focus on task-relevant regions, and shift attention towards uninformative tokens. We further demonstrate that this attention misallocation is the principal cause of degraded accuracy and cross-framing inconsistency. Building on this mechanistic insight, we introduce a lightweight prompt-tuning method using learnable tokens that encourages the robust, visually grounded attention patterns observed in open-ended settings, improving visual grounding and improving performance across framings.

</details>


### [148] [RPiAE: A Representation-Pivoted Autoencoder Enhancing Both Image Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19206)
*Yue Gong,Hongyu Li,Shanyuan Liu,Bo Cheng,Yuhang Ma,Liebucha Wu,Xiaoyu Wu,Manyuan Zhang,Dawei Leng,Yuhui Yin,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RPiAE是一种基于表示的视觉分词器，通过表示枢轴正则化和变分桥接技术，在保持预训练表示语义的同时提高重建保真度，并生成紧凑潜在空间以改善扩散建模


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预训练视觉表示模型的分词器方法存在两个主要问题：1) 冻结编码器导致重建保真度有限，进而影响编辑质量；2) 潜在空间维度过高，使扩散建模变得困难

Method: 提出表示枢轴自编码器(RPiAE)，包含三个关键技术：1) 表示枢轴正则化，允许表示初始化的编码器微调以改进重建，同时保持预训练表示空间的语义结构；2) 变分桥接，将潜在空间压缩为紧凑表示；3) 目标解耦的分阶段训练策略，顺序优化生成可处理性和重建保真度目标

Result: 实验表明，RPiAE在文本到图像生成和图像编辑任务上优于其他视觉分词器，同时在基于表示的分词器中提供最佳的重建保真度

Conclusion: RPiAE通过创新的训练策略和架构设计，解决了现有表示基分词器在重建保真度和扩散建模复杂性方面的限制，实现了在保持强语义的同时提高生成和编辑性能的平衡

Abstract: Diffusion models have become the dominant paradigm for image generation and editing, with latent diffusion models shifting denoising to a compact latent space for efficiency and scalability. Recent attempts to leverage pretrained visual representation models as tokenizer priors either align diffusion features to representation features or directly reuse representation encoders as frozen tokenizers. Although such approaches can improve generation metrics, they often suffer from limited reconstruction fidelity due to frozen encoders, which in turn degrades editing quality, as well as overly high-dimensional latents that make diffusion modeling difficult. To address these limitations, We propose Representation-Pivoted AutoEncoder, a representation-based tokenizer that improves both generation and editing. We introduce Representation-Pivot Regularization, a training strategy that enables a representation-initialized encoder to be fine-tuned for reconstruction while preserving the semantic structure of the pretrained representation space, followed by a variational bridge which compress latent space into a compact one for better diffusion modeling. We adopt an objective-decoupled stage-wise training strategy that sequentially optimizes generative tractability and reconstruction-fidelity objectives. Together, these components yield a tokenizer that preserves strong semantics, reconstructs faithfully, and produces latents with reduced diffusion modeling complexity. Experiments demonstrate that RPiAE outperforms other visual tokenizers on text-to-image generation and image editing, while delivering the best reconstruction fidelity among representation-based tokenizers.

</details>


### [149] [DreamPartGen: Semantically Grounded Part-Level 3D Generation via Collaborative Latent Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19216)
*Tianjiao Yu,Xinzhuo Li,Muntasir Wahed,Jerry Xiong,Yifan Shen,Ying Shen,Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamPartGen是一个语义基础、部件感知的文本到3D生成框架，通过双工部件潜变量和关系语义潜变量实现部件级几何与外观建模及部件间语义关系捕捉，在几何保真度和文本-形状对齐方面达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到3D方法大多忽略了部件的语义和功能结构，而最近的部件感知方法虽然引入了分解，但仍主要关注几何，缺乏语义基础，未能建模部件如何与文本描述对齐或部件间关系。

Method: 提出DreamPartGen框架，包含：1) 双工部件潜变量(DPLs)联合建模每个部件的几何和外观；2) 关系语义潜变量(RSLs)从语言中捕捉部件间依赖关系；3) 同步协同去噪过程强制几何和语义一致性。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，DreamPartGen在几何保真度和文本-形状对齐方面实现了最先进的性能，能够生成连贯、可解释且与文本对齐的3D合成结果。

Conclusion: DreamPartGen通过语义基础的部件感知生成，解决了现有方法缺乏语义结构和部件间关系建模的问题，实现了更符合人类感知和推理的3D对象生成。

Abstract: Understanding and generating 3D objects as compositions of meaningful parts is fundamental to human perception and reasoning. However, most text-to-3D methods overlook the semantic and functional structure of parts. While recent part-aware approaches introduce decomposition, they remain largely geometry-focused, lacking semantic grounding and failing to model how parts align with textual descriptions or their inter-part relations. We propose DreamPartGen, a framework for semantically grounded, part-aware text-to-3D generation. DreamPartGen introduces Duplex Part Latents (DPLs) that jointly model each part's geometry and appearance, and Relational Semantic Latents (RSLs) that capture inter-part dependencies derived from language. A synchronized co-denoising process enforces mutual geometric and semantic consistency, enabling coherent, interpretable, and text-aligned 3D synthesis. Across multiple benchmarks, DreamPartGen delivers state-of-the-art performance in geometric fidelity and text-shape alignment.

</details>


### [150] [LVOmniBench: Pioneering Long Audio-Video Understanding Evaluation for Omnimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19217)
*Keda Tao,Yuhua Zheng,Jia Xu,Wenjie Du,Kele Shao,Hesong Wang,Xueyi Chen,Xin Jin,Junhan Zhu,Bohan Yu,Weiqiang Wang,Jian Liu,Can Qin,Yulun Zhang,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Huan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LVOmniBench是一个专门评估全模态大语言模型在长音频视频理解能力的新基准，包含275个10-90分钟视频和1014个QA对，发现现有模型在处理长音频视频输入时面临显著挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前全模态大语言模型的评估主要关注10秒到5分钟的短音频视频片段，无法反映实际应用中通常持续数十分钟的长视频需求，存在评估与现实应用之间的差距。

Method: 从开放平台收集高质量视频，通过严格的人工筛选和标注构建LVOmniBench数据集，包含275个时长10-90分钟的视频和1014个问答对，旨在评估模型在长期记忆、时间定位、细粒度理解和多模态感知等方面的能力。

Result: 评估显示当前全模态大语言模型在处理长音频视频输入时面临显著挑战：开源模型准确率普遍低于35%，而Gemini 3 Pro最高达到约65%的准确率。

Conclusion: LVOmniBench基准及其实证发现将促进进一步研究，推动开发能够解决长音频视频上下文中复杂跨模态理解问题的先进模型。

Abstract: Recent advancements in omnimodal large language models (OmniLLMs) have significantly improved the comprehension of audio and video inputs. However, current evaluations primarily focus on short audio and video clips ranging from 10 seconds to 5 minutes, failing to reflect the demands of real-world applications, where videos typically run for tens of minutes. To address this critical gap, we introduce LVOmniBench, a new benchmark designed specifically for the cross-modal comprehension of long-form audio and video. This dataset comprises high-quality videos sourced from open platforms that feature rich audio-visual dynamics. Through rigorous manual selection and annotation, LVOmniBench comprises 275 videos, ranging in duration from 10 to 90 minutes, and 1,014 question-answer (QA) pairs. LVOmniBench aims to rigorously evaluate the capabilities of OmniLLMs across domains, including long-term memory, temporal localization, fine-grained understanding, and multimodal perception. Our extensive evaluation reveals that current OmniLLMs encounter significant challenges when processing extended audio-visual inputs. Open-source models generally achieve accuracies below 35%, whereas the Gemini 3 Pro reaches a peak accuracy of approximately 65%. We anticipate that this dataset, along with our empirical findings, will stimulate further research and the development of advanced models capable of resolving complex cross-modal understanding problems within long-form audio-visual contexts.

</details>


### [151] [Rethinking Vector Field Learning for Generative Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19218)
*Chaoyang Wang,Yaobo Liang,Boci Peng,Fan Duan,Jingdong Wang,Yunhai Tong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种改进扩散模型用于生成式分割的方法，通过向量场重塑策略解决流匹配目标与离散感知任务之间的不匹配问题，显著提升了生成式分割的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要关注架构调整或训练启发式方法，对连续流匹配目标与离散感知任务之间的内在不匹配理解有限。作者从向量场学习的角度重新审视扩散分割，识别出常用流匹配目标的两个关键限制：梯度消失和轨迹穿越，导致收敛缓慢和类别分离不佳。

Method: 1. 提出原则性的向量场重塑策略，通过添加分离的距离感知校正项来增强学习的速度场，引入吸引和排斥相互作用，增强质心附近的梯度幅度，同时保持原始扩散训练框架。
2. 设计计算高效的准随机类别编码方案，受Kronecker序列启发，与端到端像素神经场框架无缝集成，实现像素级语义对齐。

Result: 大量实验一致表明，相比原始流匹配方法有显著改进，大幅缩小了生成式分割与强判别式专业模型之间的性能差距。

Conclusion: 通过向量场重塑策略和准随机类别编码，有效解决了扩散分割中的梯度消失和轨迹穿越问题，为生成式分割提供了更高效和性能更好的解决方案。

Abstract: Taming diffusion models for generative segmentation has attracted increasing attention. While existing approaches primarily focus on architectural tweaks or training heuristics, there remains a limited understanding of the intrinsic mismatch between continuous flow matching objectives and discrete perception tasks. In this work, we revisit diffusion segmentation from the perspective of vector field learning. We identify two key limitations of the commonly used flow matching objective: gradient vanishing and trajectory traversing, which result in slow convergence and poor class separation. To tackle these issues, we propose a principled vector field reshaping strategy that augments the learned velocity field with a detached distance-aware correction term. This correction introduces both attractive and repulsive interactions, enhancing gradient magnitudes near centroids while preserving the original diffusion training framework. Furthermore, we design a computationally efficient, quasi-random category encoding scheme inspired by Kronecker sequences, which integrates seamlessly with an end-to-end pixel neural field framework for pixel-level semantic alignment. Extensive experiments consistently demonstrate significant improvements over vanilla flow matching approaches, substantially narrowing the performance gap between generative segmentation and strong discriminative specialists.

</details>


### [152] [DriveTok: 3D Driving Scene Tokenization for Unified Multi-View Reconstruction and Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19219)
*Dong Zhuo,Wenzhao Zheng,Sicheng Zuo,Siming Yan,Lu Hou,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriveTok：面向自动驾驶场景的高效3D多视角图像标记器，通过3D可变形交叉注意力实现统一的多视角重建与理解


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像标记器主要针对单目2D场景设计，在处理高分辨率多视角驾驶场景时存在效率低下和视角间不一致的问题，需要专门针对自动驾驶场景的3D多视角标记器

Method: 首先从视觉基础模型获取语义丰富的视觉特征，然后通过3D可变形交叉注意力将其转换为场景标记。解码时使用多视角Transformer从场景标记重建多视角特征，并通过多头机制实现RGB、深度和语义重建。还添加了3D头部直接在场景标记上进行3D语义占据预测

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上的广泛实验表明，DriveTok的场景标记在图像重建、语义分割、深度预测和3D占据预测任务上表现良好

Conclusion: DriveTok通过学习统一场景标记，集成了语义、几何和纹理信息，实现了高效的多视角标记化，为自动驾驶系统的视觉-语言-动作模型和世界模型提供了有效的视觉模态接口

Abstract: With the growing adoption of vision-language-action models and world models in autonomous driving systems, scalable image tokenization becomes crucial as the interface for the visual modality. However, most existing tokenizers are designed for monocular and 2D scenes, leading to inefficiency and inter-view inconsistency when applied to high-resolution multi-view driving scenes. To address this, we propose DriveTok, an efficient 3D driving scene tokenizer for unified multi-view reconstruction and understanding. DriveTok first obtains semantically rich visual features from vision foundation models and then transforms them into the scene tokens with 3D deformable cross-attention. For decoding, we employ a multi-view transformer to reconstruct multi-view features from the scene tokens and use multiple heads to obtain RGB, depth, and semantic reconstructions. We also add a 3D head directly on the scene tokens for 3D semantic occupancy prediction for better spatial awareness. With the multiple training objectives, DriveTok learns unified scene tokens that integrate semantic, geometric, and textural information for efficient multi-view tokenization. Extensive experiments on the widely used nuScenes dataset demonstrate that the scene tokens from DriveTok perform well on image reconstruction, semantic segmentation, depth prediction, and 3D occupancy prediction tasks.

</details>


### [153] [Spectrally-Guided Diffusion Noise Schedules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19222)
*Carlos Esteves,Ameesh Makadia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于图像频谱特性的每实例噪声调度方法，通过理论推导最小最大噪声水平边界，设计"紧凑"噪声调度消除冗余步骤，提升像素扩散模型生成质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去噪扩散模型的性能依赖于噪声调度，传统方法通常需要手动调整且对不同分辨率需要不同配置，缺乏理论指导。需要一种基于图像特性的原则性方法来设计噪声调度

Method: 基于图像频谱特性设计每实例噪声调度，推导最小和最大噪声水平的理论边界，设计"紧凑"噪声调度消除冗余步骤，在推理过程中条件采样这些噪声调度

Result: 实验表明该方法提升了单阶段像素扩散模型的生成质量，特别是在低步数情况下表现更优

Conclusion: 基于图像频谱特性的每实例噪声调度设计是一种有效的原则性方法，能够提升扩散模型的生成性能，特别是在资源受限的低步数场景下

Abstract: Denoising diffusion models are widely used for high-quality image and video generation. Their performance depends on noise schedules, which define the distribution of noise levels applied during training and the sequence of noise levels traversed during sampling. Noise schedules are typically handcrafted and require manual tuning across different resolutions. In this work, we propose a principled way to design per-instance noise schedules for pixel diffusion, based on the image's spectral properties. By deriving theoretical bounds on the efficacy of minimum and maximum noise levels, we design ``tight'' noise schedules that eliminate redundant steps. During inference, we propose to conditionally sample such noise schedules. Experiments show that our noise schedules improve generative quality of single-stage pixel diffusion models, particularly in the low-step regime.

</details>


### [154] [EffectErase: Joint Video Object Removal and Insertion for High-Quality Effect Erasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19224)
*Yang Fu,Yike Zheng,Ziyun Dai,Henghui Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了VOR数据集和EffectErase方法，用于视频对象去除任务，特别关注消除目标对象及其视觉效应（变形、阴影、反射等），并恢复无缝背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的视频修复和对象去除方法虽然能移除对象，但难以消除对象的视觉效应（如变形、阴影、反射）并合成连贯背景。此外，缺乏系统捕捉不同环境中常见对象效应的综合数据集，限制了该领域的进展。

Method: 1. 引入VOR数据集：包含60K高质量视频对（对象存在vs不存在），涵盖5种效应类型、多种对象类别和复杂动态多对象场景。2. 提出EffectErase方法：采用互惠学习方案，将视频对象插入作为逆辅助任务；包含任务感知区域引导，专注于受影响区域并实现灵活任务切换；使用插入-去除一致性目标，鼓励效应区域和结构线索的互补行为和共享定位。

Result: 在VOR数据集上训练的EffectErase在广泛实验中表现出优越性能，能够在多样化场景中实现高质量的视频对象效应擦除。

Conclusion: VOR数据集填补了视频对象去除领域的数据空白，EffectErase方法通过互惠学习和效应感知机制有效解决了对象视觉效应的消除问题，为视频对象去除任务提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Video object removal aims to eliminate dynamic target objects and their visual effects, such as deformation, shadows, and reflections, while restoring seamless backgrounds. Recent diffusion-based video inpainting and object removal methods can remove the objects but often struggle to erase these effects and to synthesize coherent backgrounds. Beyond method limitations, progress is further hampered by the lack of a comprehensive dataset that systematically captures common object effects across varied environments for training and evaluation. To address this, we introduce VOR (Video Object Removal), a large-scale dataset that provides diverse paired videos, each consisting of one video where the target object is present with its effects and a counterpart where the object and effects are absent, with corresponding object masks. VOR contains 60K high-quality video pairs from captured and synthetic sources, covers five effects types, and spans a wide range of object categories as well as complex, dynamic multi-object scenes. Building on VOR, we propose EffectErase, an effect-aware video object removal method that treats video object insertion as the inverse auxiliary task within a reciprocal learning scheme. The model includes task-aware region guidance that focuses learning on affected areas and enables flexible task switching. Then, an insertion-removal consistency objective that encourages complementary behaviors and shared localization of effect regions and structural cues. Trained on VOR, EffectErase achieves superior performance in extensive experiments, delivering high-quality video object effect erasing across diverse scenarios.

</details>


### [155] [Under One Sun: Multi-Object Generative Perception of Materials and Illumination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19226)
*Nobuo Yoshii,Xinran Nicole Han,Ryo Kawahara,Todd Zickler,Ko Nishino*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MultiGP是一种生成式逆渲染方法，可从单张图像中随机采样所有辐射成分（反射率、纹理和光照），通过利用场景中所有物体共享相同光照的特性来解决辐射解耦的固有歧义问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张图像中解耦辐射成分（反射率、纹理和光照）是一个固有歧义问题。现有方法难以同时准确恢复所有成分，特别是当场景包含多个具有不同反射率和纹理的物体时。MultiGP的动机是利用场景中所有物体共享相同光照这一事实，通过多物体之间的互补信息来约束解耦过程。

Method: MultiGP采用级联端到端架构，结合图像空间和角度空间解耦。关键技术包括：1）协调引导确保扩散收敛到一致的光照估计；2）轴向注意力促进不同反射率物体之间的"交叉对话"；3）纹理提取ControlNet在保留高频纹理细节的同时确保与估计光照的解耦。

Result: 实验结果表明，MultiGP能够有效利用多个物体外观的互补空间和频率特性，成功恢复单个物体的纹理和反射率以及共同的光照。该方法在辐射成分解耦方面表现出色，特别是在处理多物体场景时。

Conclusion: MultiGP通过利用场景中物体共享光照的共识，提出了一种有效的生成式逆渲染方法，能够从单张图像中随机采样所有辐射成分。该方法通过协调引导、轴向注意力和纹理提取ControlNet等技术，解决了辐射解耦的固有歧义问题，为计算机视觉和图形学中的逆渲染任务提供了新思路。

Abstract: We introduce Multi-Object Generative Perception (MultiGP), a generative inverse rendering method for stochastic sampling of all radiometric constituents -- reflectance, texture, and illumination -- underlying object appearance from a single image. Our key idea to solve this inherently ambiguous radiometric disentanglement is to leverage the fact that while their texture and reflectance may differ, objects in the same scene are all lit by the same illumination. MultiGP exploits this consensus to produce samples of reflectance, texture, and illumination from a single image of known shapes based on four key technical contributions: a cascaded end-to-end architecture that combines image-space and angular-space disentanglement; Coordinated Guidance for diffusion convergence to a single consistent illumination estimate; Axial Attention applied to facilitate ``cross-talk'' between objects of different reflectance; and a Texture Extraction ControlNet to preserve high-frequency texture details while ensuring decoupling from estimated lighting. Experimental results demonstrate that MultiGP effectively leverages the complementary spatial and frequency characteristics of multiple object appearances to recover individual texture and reflectance as well as the common illumination.

</details>


### [156] [Bridging Semantic and Kinematic Conditions with Diffusion-based Discrete Motion Tokenizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19227)
*Chenyang Gu,Mingyuan Zhang,Haozhe Xie,Zhongang Cai,Lei Yang,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出三阶段框架MoTok，结合离散令牌生成和连续扩散模型的优势，通过感知-规划-控制架构实现运动生成，在保持语义控制的同时显著提升运动保真度和运动学约束能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动生成方法存在局限性：连续扩散模型擅长运动学控制但语义控制能力有限，离散令牌生成器在语义条件方面有效但运动学控制不足。需要结合两者优势，实现既保持语义控制又具备精细运动学约束能力的运动生成方法。

Method: 提出三阶段框架：1) 感知阶段提取条件特征；2) 规划阶段生成离散运动令牌(MoTok)；3) 控制阶段使用扩散模型合成运动。MoTok作为核心组件，将语义抽象与精细重建解耦，通过扩散解码器恢复运动，使用紧凑的单层令牌。运动学约束分为粗粒度（规划阶段引导令牌生成）和细粒度（控制阶段通过扩散优化强制执行），防止运动学细节干扰语义令牌规划。

Result: 在HumanML3D数据集上显著提升可控性和保真度：相比MaskControl方法，使用仅1/6的令牌数量，轨迹误差从0.72cm降至0.08cm，FID从0.083降至0.029。在更强运动学约束下，先前方法性能下降，而本方法FID从0.033进一步降至0.014，表现出更强的约束适应能力。

Conclusion: 提出的三阶段框架成功结合了离散令牌生成和连续扩散模型的优势，通过解耦语义抽象与运动重建，实现了高效的运动生成。MoTok令牌化方法在保持运动保真度的同时显著减少了令牌数量，分层运动学约束策略有效防止了细节干扰语义规划，在运动学约束增强时仍能提升生成质量。

Abstract: Prior motion generation largely follows two paradigms: continuous diffusion models that excel at kinematic control, and discrete token-based generators that are effective for semantic conditioning. To combine their strengths, we propose a three-stage framework comprising condition feature extraction (Perception), discrete token generation (Planning), and diffusion-based motion synthesis (Control). Central to this framework is MoTok, a diffusion-based discrete motion tokenizer that decouples semantic abstraction from fine-grained reconstruction by delegating motion recovery to a diffusion decoder, enabling compact single-layer tokens while preserving motion fidelity. For kinematic conditions, coarse constraints guide token generation during planning, while fine-grained constraints are enforced during control through diffusion-based optimization. This design prevents kinematic details from disrupting semantic token planning. On HumanML3D, our method significantly improves controllability and fidelity over MaskControl while using only one-sixth of the tokens, reducing trajectory error from 0.72 cm to 0.08 cm and FID from 0.083 to 0.029. Unlike prior methods that degrade under stronger kinematic constraints, ours improves fidelity, reducing FID from 0.033 to 0.014.

</details>


### [157] [SAMA: Factorized Semantic Anchoring and Motion Alignment for Instruction-Guided Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19228)
*Xinyao Zhang,Wenkai Dong,Yuxin Song,Bo Fang,Qi Zhang,Jing Wang,Fan Chen,Hui Zhang,Haocheng Feng,Yu Lu,Hang Zhou,Chun Yuan,Jingdong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAMA框架通过分解语义锚定和运动对齐来解决视频编辑中语义修改与运动保持的平衡问题，无需外部先验，在零样本和微调后均取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于指令的视频编辑模型难以同时平衡精确的语义修改和忠实的运动保持。现有方法依赖外部先验（如VLM特征或结构条件），这严重限制了模型的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出SAMA框架，将视频编辑分解为语义锚定和运动建模。语义锚定在稀疏锚定帧上联合预测语义标记和视频潜在表示，实现纯指令感知的结构规划。运动对齐通过视频修复预训练任务（立方体修复、速度扰动、管状混洗）使模型直接从原始视频内化时间动态。采用两阶段优化：分解预训练阶段学习内在的语义-运动表示（无需配对编辑数据），然后在配对编辑数据上进行监督微调。

Result: 分解预训练本身已展现出强大的零样本视频编辑能力，验证了分解方法的有效性。SAMA在开源模型中达到最先进的性能，并与领先的商业系统（如Kling-Omni）竞争。代码、模型和数据集将发布。

Conclusion: SAMA通过分解语义锚定和运动对齐，解决了视频编辑中语义修改与运动保持的平衡问题，无需依赖外部先验，在零样本和微调后均表现出色，为视频编辑提供了新的有效框架。

Abstract: Current instruction-guided video editing models struggle to simultaneously balance precise semantic modifications with faithful motion preservation. While existing approaches rely on injecting explicit external priors (e.g., VLM features or structural conditions) to mitigate these issues, this reliance severely bottlenecks model robustness and generalization. To overcome this limitation, we present SAMA (factorized Semantic Anchoring and Motion Alignment), a framework that factorizes video editing into semantic anchoring and motion modeling. First, we introduce Semantic Anchoring, which establishes a reliable visual anchor by jointly predicting semantic tokens and video latents at sparse anchor frames, enabling purely instruction-aware structural planning. Second, Motion Alignment pre-trains the same backbone on motion-centric video restoration pretext tasks (cube inpainting, speed perturbation, and tube shuffle), enabling the model to internalize temporal dynamics directly from raw videos. SAMA is optimized with a two-stage pipeline: a factorized pre-training stage that learns inherent semantic-motion representations without paired video-instruction editing data, followed by supervised fine-tuning on paired editing data. Remarkably, the factorized pre-training alone already yields strong zero-shot video editing ability, validating the proposed factorization. SAMA achieves state-of-the-art performance among open-source models and is competitive with leading commercial systems (e.g., Kling-Omni). Code, models, and datasets will be released.

</details>


### [158] [MonoArt: Progressive Structural Reasoning for Monocular Articulated 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19231)
*Haitian Li,Haozhe Xie,Junxiang Xu,Beichen Wen,Fangzhou Hong,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MonoArt是一个从单图像重建关节3D物体的统一框架，通过渐进式结构推理将视觉观察转化为规范几何、结构化部件表示和运动感知嵌入，实现稳定且可解释的关节推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单图像重建关节3D物体需要从有限的视觉证据中联合推断几何、部件结构和运动参数。关键困难在于运动线索和物体结构之间的纠缠，这使得直接回归关节不稳定。现有方法通过多视角监督、基于检索的组装或辅助视频生成来解决这一挑战，但往往牺牲了可扩展性或效率。

Method: MonoArt采用渐进式结构推理的统一框架。不是直接从图像特征预测关节，而是逐步将视觉观察转化为规范几何、结构化部件表示和运动感知嵌入，所有这些都在单一架构内完成。这种结构化推理过程无需外部运动模板或多阶段流程。

Result: 在PartNet-Mobility数据集上的广泛实验表明，MonoArt在重建精度和推理速度方面都达到了最先进的性能。该框架进一步推广到机器人操作和关节场景重建任务中。

Conclusion: MonoArt通过渐进式结构推理提供了一种稳定、可解释且高效的关节3D物体重建方法，解决了现有方法在可扩展性和效率方面的限制，并在多个应用场景中展示了良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Reconstructing articulated 3D objects from a single image requires jointly inferring object geometry, part structure, and motion parameters from limited visual evidence. A key difficulty lies in the entanglement between motion cues and object structure, which makes direct articulation regression unstable. Existing methods address this challenge through multi-view supervision, retrieval-based assembly, or auxiliary video generation, often sacrificing scalability or efficiency. We present MonoArt, a unified framework grounded in progressive structural reasoning. Rather than predicting articulation directly from image features, MonoArt progressively transforms visual observations into canonical geometry, structured part representations, and motion-aware embeddings within a single architecture. This structured reasoning process enables stable and interpretable articulation inference without external motion templates or multi-stage pipelines. Extensive experiments on PartNet-Mobility demonstrate that OM achieves state-of-the-art performance in both reconstruction accuracy and inference speed. The framework further generalizes to robotic manipulation and articulated scene reconstruction.

</details>


### [159] [Cubic Discrete Diffusion: Discrete Visual Generation on High-Dimensional Representation Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19232)
*Yuqing Wang,Chuofan Ma,Zhijie Lin,Yao Teng,Lijun Yu,Shuai Wang,Jiaming Han,Jiashi Feng,Yi Jiang,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CubiD是首个用于高维表示（768-1024维）的离散生成模型，通过细粒度掩码实现跨维度和空间位置的丰富相关性学习，在ImageNet-256上取得SOTA离散生成性能，同时保持原始表示能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前离散生成方法局限于低维潜在token（8-32维），牺牲了理解所需的语义丰富性。虽然高维预训练表示（768-1024维）可以弥合这一差距，但对其进行离散生成存在根本性挑战。需要开发能够处理高维表示的离散生成模型，以实现统一的多模态架构。

Method: 提出Cubic Discrete Diffusion (CubiD)，在高维离散表示中执行细粒度掩码——任何位置、任何维度都可以被掩码并从部分观察中预测。这使得模型能够学习空间位置内部和之间的丰富相关性，生成步骤固定为T（T≪hwd），与特征维度无关。

Result: 在ImageNet-256上，CubiD实现了最先进的离散生成性能，从9亿到37亿参数都表现出强大的扩展性。关键验证表明这些离散化token保留了原始表示能力，同一离散token可以同时有效服务于理解和生成任务。

Conclusion: CubiD是首个高维表示的离散生成模型，通过细粒度掩码机制实现了丰富的相关性学习，在保持表示能力的同时取得了优异的生成性能，为统一的多模态架构研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Visual generation with discrete tokens has gained significant attention as it enables a unified token prediction paradigm shared with language models, promising seamless multimodal architectures. However, current discrete generation methods remain limited to low-dimensional latent tokens (typically 8-32 dims), sacrificing the semantic richness essential for understanding. While high-dimensional pretrained representations (768-1024 dims) could bridge this gap, their discrete generation poses fundamental challenges. In this paper, we present Cubic Discrete Diffusion (CubiD), the first discrete generation model for high-dimensional representations. CubiD performs fine-grained masking throughout the high-dimensional discrete representation -- any dimension at any position can be masked and predicted from partial observations. This enables the model to learn rich correlations both within and across spatial positions, with the number of generation steps fixed at $T$ regardless of feature dimensionality, where $T \ll hwd$. On ImageNet-256, CubiD achieves state-of-the-art discrete generation with strong scaling behavior from 900M to 3.7B parameters. Crucially, we validate that these discretized tokens preserve original representation capabilities, demonstrating that the same discrete tokens can effectively serve both understanding and generation tasks. We hope this work will inspire future research toward unified multimodal architectures. Code is available at: https://github.com/YuqingWang1029/CubiD.

</details>


### [160] [Generation Models Know Space: Unleashing Implicit 3D Priors for Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19235)
*Xianjin Wu,Dingkang Liang,Tianrui Feng,Kui Xia,Yumeng Zhang,Xiaofan Li,Xiao Tan,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VEGA-3D利用视频生成模型的隐式空间先验，通过提取时空特征增强多模态大语言模型的几何推理能力，无需显式3D监督


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型存在空间盲区，难以进行细粒度几何推理和物理动态理解。现有方法依赖显式3D模态或复杂几何支架，受限于数据稀缺和泛化挑战

Method: 提出VEGA-3D框架，将预训练视频扩散模型重新用作潜在世界模拟器。通过提取中间噪声级别的时空特征，并通过令牌级自适应门控融合机制与语义表示集成，为MLLM提供密集几何线索

Result: 在3D场景理解、空间推理和具身操作基准测试中，该方法优于最先进的基线方法，验证了生成先验为物理世界理解提供了可扩展的基础

Conclusion: 利用大规模视频生成模型的隐式空间先验是增强多模态大语言模型几何推理能力的有效范式，无需显式3D监督，为物理世界理解提供了可扩展的解决方案

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models demonstrate impressive semantic capabilities, they often suffer from spatial blindness, struggling with fine-grained geometric reasoning and physical dynamics. Existing solutions typically rely on explicit 3D modalities or complex geometric scaffolding, which are limited by data scarcity and generalization challenges. In this work, we propose a paradigm shift by leveraging the implicit spatial prior within large-scale video generation models. We posit that to synthesize temporally coherent videos, these models inherently learn robust 3D structural priors and physical laws. We introduce VEGA-3D (Video Extracted Generative Awareness), a plug-and-play framework that repurposes a pre-trained video diffusion model as a Latent World Simulator. By extracting spatiotemporal features from intermediate noise levels and integrating them with semantic representations via a token-level adaptive gated fusion mechanism, we enrich MLLMs with dense geometric cues without explicit 3D supervision. Extensive experiments across 3D scene understanding, spatial reasoning, and embodied manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, validating that generative priors provide a scalable foundation for physical-world understanding. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/H-EmbodVis/VEGA-3D.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [Semantic Chameleon: Corpus-Dependent Poisoning Attacks and Defenses in RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18034)
*Scott Thornton*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对RAG系统的梯度引导语料库投毒攻击研究：通过优化触发文档实现检索层攻击，混合检索（BM25+向量）可显著降低攻击成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统通过外部知识源扩展LLM能力，但检索管道引入了新的攻击面。攻击者可以投毒检索语料库，使恶意文档在推理时被优先检索，从而操纵模型输出。本研究旨在探索针对现代RAG管道的梯度引导语料库投毒攻击，并评估无需修改底层LLM的检索层防御方法。

Method: 采用双文档投毒攻击策略：包含休眠文档和触发文档，使用贪婪坐标梯度（GCG）优化触发文档。在Security Stack Exchange语料库（67,941个文档）上进行大规模评估，进行50次攻击尝试。评估了纯向量检索和混合检索（BM25+向量相似度）两种配置下的攻击效果，并在五个LLM家族上测试攻击成功率。

Result: 梯度引导投毒攻击在纯向量检索下达到38.0%的共检索率。混合检索将攻击成功率从38%降至0%。当攻击者同时优化稀疏和密集检索信号时，混合检索可被部分绕过（20-44%成功率），但仍显著高于纯向量检索。在五个LLM家族上攻击成功率为46.7%至93.3%。在FEVER Wikipedia数据集上的跨语料评估中，所有检索配置下攻击成功率均为0%。

Conclusion: 梯度引导语料库投毒攻击对RAG系统构成实质性威胁，特别是在纯向量检索配置下。混合检索（结合BM25和向量相似度）作为简单的架构修改，能有效缓解此类攻击，显著提高攻击难度。研究强调了RAG系统检索层安全的重要性，并展示了无需修改LLM的实用防御方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems extend large language models (LLMs) with external knowledge sources but introduce new attack surfaces through the retrieval pipeline. In particular, adversaries can poison retrieval corpora so that malicious documents are preferentially retrieved at inference time, enabling targeted manipulation of model outputs. We study gradient-guided corpus poisoning attacks against modern RAG pipelines and evaluate retrieval-layer defenses that require no modification to the underlying LLM.
  We implement dual-document poisoning attacks consisting of a sleeper document and a trigger document optimized using Greedy Coordinate Gradient (GCG). In a large-scale evaluation on the Security Stack Exchange corpus (67,941 documents) with 50 attack attempts, gradient-guided poisoning achieves a 38.0 percent co-retrieval rate under pure vector retrieval.
  We show that a simple architectural modification, hybrid retrieval combining BM25 and vector similarity, substantially mitigates this attack. Across all 50 attacks, hybrid retrieval reduces gradient-guided attack success from 38 percent to 0 percent without modifying the model or retraining the retriever. When attackers jointly optimize payloads for both sparse and dense retrieval signals, hybrid retrieval can be partially circumvented, achieving 20-44 percent success, but still significantly raises attack difficulty relative to vector-only retrieval.
  Evaluation across five LLM families (GPT-5.3, GPT-4o, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Llama 4, and GPT-4o-mini) shows attack success ranging from 46.7 percent to 93.3 percent. Cross-corpus evaluation on the FEVER Wikipedia dataset (25 attacks) yields 0 percent attack success across all retrieval configurations.

</details>


### [162] [Guardrails as Infrastructure: Policy-First Control for Tool-Orchestrated Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18059)
*Akshey Sigdel,Rista Baral*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出Policy-First Tooling框架，通过模型无关的权限层来约束工具调用，解决自动化系统中的常见故障模式，包括不安全副作用、无效参数、不受控重试和敏感信息泄露等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工具使用自动化系统（从脚本、CI机器人到智能助手）存在重复的故障模式，包括不安全副作用、无效参数、不受控重试和敏感信息泄露。现有缓解措施多为模型中心化和提示依赖型，因此脆弱且无法泛化到非LLM调用者。

Method: 提出Policy-First Tooling框架：1) 紧凑的策略领域特定语言（DSL）；2) 运行时强制执行架构，具有可操作的原理说明和修复提示；3) 基于轨迹重放的可复现基准，包含受控故障和误用注入。

Result: 在225个受控运行中，跨越5个策略包和3个故障配置文件：更严格的策略包将违规预防从P0的0.000提升到P4的0.681；任务成功率从0.356下降到0.067；重试放大从3.774降至1.378；在注入秘密输出的情况下，泄露召回率达到0.875。

Conclusion: Policy-First Tooling框架通过明确的约束、风险感知的门控、恢复控制和可审计的解释，为工具调用提供了模型无关的权限层，使安全性与实用性之间的权衡变得明确且可测量。

Abstract: Tool-using automation systems, from scripts and CI bots to agentic assistants, fail in recurring patterns. Common failures include unsafe side effects, invalid arguments, uncontrolled retries, and leakage of sensitive outputs. Many mitigations are model-centric and prompt-dependent, so they are brittle and do not generalize to non-LLM callers. We present Policy-First Tooling, a model-agnostic permission layer that mediates tool invocation through explicit constraints, risk-aware gating, recovery controls, and auditable explanations. The paper contributes a compact policy DSL, a runtime enforcement architecture with actionable rationale and fix hints, and a reproducible benchmark based on trace replay with controlled fault and misuse injection. In 225 controlled runs across five policy packs and three fault profiles, stricter packs improve violation prevention from 0.000 in P0 to 0.681 in P4, while task success drops from 0.356 to 0.067. Retry amplification decreases from 3.774 in P0 to 1.378 in P4, and leakage recall reaches 0.875 under injected secret outputs. These results make safety to utility trade-offs explicit and measurable.

</details>


### [163] [STEP: Detecting Audio Backdoor Attacks via Stability-based Trigger Exposure Profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18103)
*Kun Wang,Meng Chen,Junhao Wang,Yuli Wu,Li Lu,Chong Zhang,Peng Cheng,Jiaheng Zhang,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: STEP是一种基于稳定性的黑盒后门检测器，利用后门触发器的双重异常特性：在语义破坏扰动下的异常标签稳定性，以及在语义保持扰动下的异常标签脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着深度学习语音模型在安全关键应用中的广泛部署，后门攻击已成为严重威胁。现有推理时防御方法不适用于音频领域，因为它们要么依赖触发器过鲁棒性假设（对基于变换和语义的触发器失效），要么依赖于图像或文本模态的特定属性。

Method: STEP是一种黑盒、无需重新训练的后门检测器，仅需硬标签访问。核心思想是利用后门触发器的双重异常特性：1) 在语义破坏扰动下的异常标签稳定性；2) 在语义保持扰动下的异常标签脆弱性。方法包括：使用两个互补的扰动分支分析每个测试样本，分别针对这两种特性；用良性参考训练的一类异常检测器对生成的稳定性特征进行评分；通过无监督加权融合两个分数。

Result: 在七种后门攻击上的广泛实验表明，STEP平均AUROC达到97.92%，EER为4.54%，显著优于最先进的基线方法，并能跨模型架构、语音任务、开放集验证场景以及空中物理世界设置进行泛化。

Conclusion: STEP通过利用后门触发器的双重异常特性，提供了一种有效的黑盒后门检测方法，适用于音频领域，无需重新训练，仅需硬标签访问，在各种攻击场景下表现出优越性能。

Abstract: With the widespread deployment of deep-learning-based speech models in security-critical applications, backdoor attacks have emerged as a serious threat: an adversary who poisons a small fraction of training data can implant a hidden trigger that controls the model's output while preserving normal behavior on clean inputs. Existing inference-time defenses are not well suited to the audio domain, as they either rely on trigger over-robustness assumptions that fail on transformation-based and semantic triggers, or depend on properties specific to image or text modalities. In this paper, we propose STEP (Stability-based Trigger Exposure Profiling), a black-box, retraining-free backdoor detector that operates under hard-label-only access. Its core idea is to exploit a characteristic dual anomaly of backdoor triggers: anomalous label stability under semantic-breaking perturbations, and anomalous label fragility under semantic-preserving perturbations. STEP profiles each test sample with two complementary perturbation branches that target these two properties respectively, scores the resulting stability features with one-class anomaly detectors trained on benign references, and fuses the two scores via unsupervised weighting. Extensive experiments across seven backdoor attacks show that STEP achieves an average AUROC of 97.92% and EER of 4.54%, substantially outperforming state-of-the-art baselines, and generalizes across model architectures, speech tasks, an open-set verification scenario, and over-the-air physical-world settings.

</details>


### [164] [Adaptive Fuzzy Logic-Based Steganographic Encryption Framework: A Comprehensive Experimental Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18105)
*Aadi Joshi,Kavya Bhand*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应图像隐写框架，结合模糊推理系统和现代认证加密，根据局部熵、边缘幅度和载荷压力动态确定像素级嵌入深度（1-3位），同时保持编码器-解码器同步，并通过加密层保护载荷机密性和完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定深度最低有效位嵌入方法在平滑区和纹理区采用统一修改策略，导致失真增加和统计可检测性提升。需要一种自适应方法在保持高容量的同时，根据图像局部统计特性动态调整嵌入深度，以平衡载荷容量、视觉保真度和统计不可检测性。

Method: 1. 基于Mamdani型模糊推理系统构建自适应控制机制，以局部熵、边缘幅度和载荷压力作为语言输入，确定像素级嵌入深度（1-3位）。2. 从低位剥离图像计算相同特征图，使自适应控制机制对嵌入过程中引入的最低有效位修改保持不变，保持编码器-解码器同步。3. 采用基于Argon2id和AES-256-GCM的加密层独立保护载荷机密性和完整性。

Result: 该方法实现了像素级自适应嵌入深度控制，能够在不同图像区域采用不同嵌入强度，相比固定深度方法减少平滑区域的失真和统计可检测性，同时保持高容量。加密层确保载荷安全独立于隐写隐蔽性。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应隐写框架通过模糊推理系统实现智能嵌入深度控制，结合现代加密技术提供多层次安全保护，在保持传统方法简单性和高容量优势的同时，显著改善了视觉保真度和统计不可检测性之间的权衡。

Abstract: Digital image steganography requires a careful trade-off among payload capacity, visual fidelity, and statistical undetectability. Fixed-depth least significant bit embedding remains attractive because of its simplicity and high capacity, but it modifies smooth and textured regions uniformly, thereby increasing distortion and detectability in statistically sensitive areas. This paper presents an adaptive steganographic framework that combines a Mamdanitype fuzzy inference system with modern authenticated encryption. The proposed method determines a pixel-wise embedding depth from 1 to 3 bits using local entropy, edge magnitude, and payload pressure as linguistic inputs. To preserve encoder-decoder synchronization, the same feature maps are computed from lower-bit-stripped images, making the adaptive control mechanism invariant to the least significant modifications introduced during embedding. A cryptographic layer based on Argon2id and AES-256-GCM protects payload confidentiality and integrity independently of steganographic concealment.

</details>


### [165] [MAED: Mathematical Activation Error Detection for Mitigating Physical Fault Attacks in DNN Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18120)
*Kasra Ahmadi,Saeed Aghapour,Mehran Mozaffari Kermani,Reza Azarderakhsh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MAED是一种算法级错误检测框架，利用数学恒等式实时验证DNN中非线性激活函数计算的正确性，防御恶意故障注入攻击和自然故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式系统中DNN推理阶段容易受到故障攻击和故障影响，可能导致错误预测并产生灾难性后果，需要有效的缓解技术。

Method: 提出MAED框架，利用数学恒等式在运行时持续验证非线性激活函数（ReLu、sigmoid、tanh）计算的正确性，通过算法级错误检测技术防御故障。

Result: 通过故障模型模拟评估，实现接近100%的错误检测率，有效缓解现有DNN推理故障攻击。在ATmega328P微控制器上时钟周期开销小于1%，在AMD/Xilinx Artix-7 FPGA上几乎不增加额外面积，sigmoid和tanh的延迟增加约20%。

Conclusion: MAED是首个集成算法级错误检测技术来防御嵌入式系统中关键DNN组件恶意故障注入攻击和自然故障的框架，具有高效性和实用性。

Abstract: The inference phase of deep neural networks (DNNs) in embedded systems is increasingly vulnerable to fault attacks and failures, which can result in incorrect predictions. These vulnerabilities can potentially lead to catastrophic consequences, making the development of effective mitigation techniques essential. In this paper, we introduce MAED (Mathematical Activation Error Detection), an algorithm-level error detection framework that exploits mathematical identities to continuously validate the correctness of non-linear activation function computations at runtime. To the best of our knowledge, this work is the first to integrate algorithm-level error detection techniques to defend against both malicious fault injection attacks and naturally occurring faults in critical DNN components in embedded systems. The evaluation is conducted on three widely adopted activation functions, namely ReLu, sigmoid, and tanh which serve as fundamental building blocks for introducing non-linearity in DNNs and can lead to mispredictions when subjected to natural faults or fault attacks. We assessed the proposed error detection scheme via fault model simulation, achieving close to 100% error detection while mitigating existing fault attacks on DNN inference. Additionally, the overhead introduced by integrating the proposed scheme with the baseline implementation (i.e., without error detection) is validated through implementations on an AMD/Xilinx Artix-7 FPGA and an ATmega328P microcontroller, as well as through integration with TensorFlow. On the microcontroller, the proposed error detection incurs less than 1% clock cycle overhead, while on the FPGA it requires nearly zero additional area, at the cost of approximately a 20% increase in latency for sigmoid and tanh.

</details>


### [166] [Toward Reliable, Safe, and Secure LLMs for Scientific Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18235)
*Saket Sanjeev Chaturvedi,Joshua Bergerson,Tanwi Mallick*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了大型语言模型作为"AI科学家"在科学应用中的安全挑战，提出了针对科学领域LLM威胁的分类法、自动化安全基准生成机制和多层防御框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM演变为自主的"AI科学家"，它们在科学领域带来了变革性进展，但也引入了新的安全漏洞，包括潜在的"生物安全风险"和"危险爆炸"等。现有的通用安全基准存在领域不匹配、威胁覆盖有限和基准过拟合等问题，无法有效评估科学应用中的漏洞，这为可信部署创造了关键缺口。

Method: 1. 为科学研究背景下的LLM威胁构建详细分类法，以更好地理解科学领域LLM的独特风险；2. 概念化一种机制，利用专用的多智能体系统自动生成领域特定的对抗性安全基准；3. 提出一个概念性的多层防御框架，将红队演练和外部边界控制与主动的内部安全LLM智能体相结合。

Result: 论文提出了三个核心概念元素：1) 针对科学领域LLM威胁的详细分类法；2) 自动化生成领域特定安全基准的机制；3) 结合红队演练、外部控制和内部安全智能体的多层防御框架。这些元素共同为科学领域可信LLM智能体部署提供了定义、评估和创建全面防御策略的必要结构。

Conclusion: 为确保LLM在科学领域的可信部署，需要建立以可靠性、安全性和安全性为核心的新范式。论文提出的威胁分类法、自动化基准生成机制和多层防御框架为解决科学应用中的独特安全挑战提供了必要的概念结构，填补了现有评估方法的空白。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) evolve into autonomous "AI scientists," they promise transformative advances but introduce novel vulnerabilities, from potential "biosafety risks" to "dangerous explosions." Ensuring trustworthy deployment in science requires a new paradigm centered on reliability (ensuring factual accuracy and reproducibility), safety (preventing unintentional physical or biological harm), and security (preventing malicious misuse). Existing general-purpose safety benchmarks are poorly suited for this purpose, suffering from a fundamental domain mismatch, limited threat coverage of science-specific vectors, and benchmark overfitting, which create a critical gap in vulnerability evaluation for scientific applications. This paper examines the unique security and safety landscape of LLM agents in science. We begin by synthesizing a detailed taxonomy of LLM threats contextualized for scientific research, to better understand the unique risks associated with LLMs in science. Next, we conceptualize a mechanism to address the evaluation gap by utilizing dedicated multi-agent systems for the automated generation of domain-specific adversarial security benchmarks. Based on our analysis, we outline how existing safety methods can be brought together and integrated into a conceptual multilayered defense framework designed to combine a red-teaming exercise and external boundary controls with a proactive internal Safety LLM Agent. Together, these conceptual elements provide a necessary structure for defining, evaluating, and creating comprehensive defense strategies for trustworthy LLM agent deployment in scientific disciplines.

</details>


### [167] [Pushan: Trace-Free Deobfuscation of Virtualization-Obfuscated Binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18355)
*Ashwin Sudhir,Zion Leonahenahe Basque,Wil Gibbs,Ati Priya Bajaj,Pulkit Singh Singaria,Mitchell Zakocs,Jie Hu,Moritz Schloegel,Tiffany Bao,Adam Doupe,Yan Shoshitaishvili,Ruoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PUSHAN是一种创新的虚拟化混淆二进制去混淆技术，无需执行轨迹，使用约束无关符号模拟恢复完整CFG，并能生成高质量C伪代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前虚拟化混淆技术是恶意软件分析的主要障碍。现有自动化去混淆方法存在三大缺陷：1) 仅适用于执行轨迹，无法恢复所有逻辑；2) 依赖昂贵的动态符号执行，扩展性差；3) 无法生成"格式良好"的代码，影响二进制反编译器的可读性输出。

Method: PUSHAN采用无轨迹方法，避免路径约束累积，使用VPC敏感、约束无关的符号模拟来恢复虚拟化函数的完整控制流图(CFG)。这是首个能够将受保护代码反编译为高质量C伪代码的方法，并规避了路径可满足性这一NP难问题的依赖。

Result: 在超过1000个二进制文件上评估PUSHAN，包括学术最先进(Tigress)和商业级混淆器(VMProtect和Themida)保护的目标。PUSHAN成功去混淆这些二进制文件，检索其完整CFG，并将其反编译为C伪代码。进一步通过分析VirusTotal中先前未分析的VMProtect混淆恶意软件样本，展示了其适用性，反编译输出支持LLM辅助的代码简化、重用和程序理解。

Conclusion: PUSHAN是一种通用且有效的虚拟化混淆二进制去混淆技术，克服了现有方法的局限性，能够恢复完整CFG并生成高质量反编译代码，为恶意软件分析提供了强大的自动化工具。

Abstract: In the ever-evolving battle against malware, binary obfuscation techniques are a formidable barrier to effective analysis by both human security analysts and automated systems. In particular, virtualization or VM-based obfuscation is one of the strongest protection mechanisms that evade automated analysis. Despite widespread use of virtualization, existing automated deobfuscation techniques suffer from three major drawbacks. First, they only work on execution traces, which prevents them from recovering all logic in an obfuscated binary. Second, they depend on dynamic symbolic execution, which is expensive and does not scale in practice. Third, they cannot generate "well-formed" code, which prevents existing binary decompilers from generating human-friendly output.
  This paper introduces PUSHAN, a novel and generic technique for deobfuscating virtualization-obfuscated binaries while overcoming the limitations of existing techniques. PUSHAN is trace-free and avoids path-constraint accumulation by using VPC-sensitive, constraint-free symbolic emulation to recover a complete CFG of the virtualized function. It is the first approach that also decompiles the protected code into high-quality C pseudocode to enable effective analysis. Crucially, PUSHAN circumvents reliance on path satisfiability, a known NP-hard problem that hampers scalability. We evaluate PUSHAN on more than 1,000 binaries, including targets protected by academic state of the art (Tigress) and commercial-strength obfuscators VMProtect and Themida. PUSHAN successfully deobfuscates these binaries, retrieves their complete CFGs, and decompiles them to C pseudocode. We further demonstrate applicability by analyzing a previously unanalyzed VMProtect-obfuscated malware sample from VirusTotal, where our decompiled output enables LLM-assisted code simplification, reuse, and program understanding.

</details>


### [168] [Prompt Control-Flow Integrity: A Priority-Aware Runtime Defense Against Prompt Injection in LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18433)
*Md Takrim Ul Alam,Akif Islam,Mohd Ruhul Ameen,Abu Saleh Musa Miah,Jungpil Shin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PCFI是一种针对LLM API和RAG系统的运行时防御机制，通过结构化建模提示请求并实施优先级感知的策略执行，有效拦截提示注入攻击，同时保持零误报率和极低延迟开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM API和RAG系统容易受到提示注入攻击，现有防御方法通常将提示视为扁平字符串，依赖临时过滤或静态越狱检测，缺乏结构化、优先级感知的运行时保护机制。

Method: 提出Prompt Control-Flow Integrity (PCFI)，将每个请求建模为系统、开发者、用户和检索文档段的结构化组合，采用三阶段中间件管道：词汇启发式检测、角色切换检测和分层策略执行，在请求转发到后端LLM前进行防护。

Result: 在自定义的合成和半真实提示注入基准测试中，PCFI拦截了所有攻击标记的请求，保持0%误报率，仅引入0.04毫秒的中位处理开销，证明其在实际部署中的有效性和轻量性。

Conclusion: 基于来源和优先级感知的提示执行是部署LLM系统的实用且轻量级防御方案，PCFI通过结构化建模和运行时检查为LLM API和RAG栈提供了有效的安全保护。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) deployed behind APIs and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) stacks are vulnerable to prompt injection attacks that may override system policies, subvert intended behavior, and induce unsafe outputs. Existing defenses often treat prompts as flat strings and rely on ad hoc filtering or static jailbreak detection. This paper proposes Prompt Control-Flow Integrity (PCFI), a priority-aware runtime defense that models each request as a structured composition of system, developer, user, and retrieved-document segments. PCFI applies a three-stage middleware pipeline, lexical heuristics, role-switch detection, and hierarchical policy enforcement, before forwarding requests to the backend LLM. We implement PCFI as a FastAPI-based gateway for deployed LLM APIs and evaluate it on a custom benchmark of synthetic and semi-realistic prompt-injection workloads. On the evaluated benchmark suite, PCFI intercepts all attack-labeled requests, maintains a 0% False Positive Rate, and introduces a median processing overhead of only 0.04 ms. These results suggest that provenance- and priority-aware prompt enforcement is a practical and lightweight defense for deployed LLM systems.

</details>


### [169] [CNT: Safety-oriented Function Reuse across LLMs via Cross-Model Neuron Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18449)
*Yue Zhao,Yujia Gong,Ruigang Liang,Shenchen Zhu,Kai Chen,Xuejing Yuan,Wangjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CNT是一种后处理方法，通过从开源供体LLM转移最小神经元子集到目标LLM，实现安全功能的重用，支持功能添加和删除，在多种安全应用中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型的广泛部署，需要灵活适应不断发展的安全要求的后处理方法。开源LLM生态系统已产生具有各种安全功能的模型，这促使从零构建安全功能转向重用现有功能，避免昂贵的数据收集和训练过程。

Method: 提出跨模型神经元转移（CNT）方法，通过从开源供体LLM转移最小神经元子集到目标LLM，在神经元级别操作，支持模块化功能级适应，包括功能添加和删除。

Result: 在7个流行LLM和3个代表性应用（安全失对齐、对齐增强和偏见移除）上的实验表明，CNT实现了有针对性的安全功能转移，性能下降最小（大多数模型小于1%），持续优于5个基线方法。

Conclusion: CNT展示了其通用性和实际有效性，为LLM安全功能适应提供了一种高效的后处理方法，能够重用现有模型的安全功能而不需要昂贵的重新训练。

Abstract: The widespread deployment of large language models (LLMs) calls for post-hoc methods that can flexibly adapt models to evolving safety requirements. Meanwhile, the rapidly expanding open-source LLM ecosystem has produced a diverse collection of models that already exhibit various safety-related functionalities. This motivates a shift from constructing safety functionality from scratch to reusing existing functionality from external models, thereby avoiding costly data collection and training procedures.
  In this paper, we present Cross-Model Neuron Transfer (CNT), a post-hoc method that reuses safety-oriented functionality by transferring a minimal subset of neurons from an open-source donor LLM to a target LLM. By operating at the neuron level, CNT enables modular function-level adaptation, supporting both function addition andfunction deletion. We evaluate CNT on seven popular LLMs across three representative applications: safety disalignment, alignment enhancement, and bias removal. Experimental results show that CNT achieves targeted safety-oriented functionality transfer with minimal performance degradation (less than 1% for most models), consistently outperforming five baselines, demonstrating its generality and practical effectiveness.

</details>


### [170] [Impact of Differentials in SIMON32 Algorithm for Lightweight Security of Internet of Things](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18455)
*Jonathan Cook,Sabih ur Rehman,M. Arif Khan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了SIMON32密码的差分特性，通过识别高概率差分提高了密码分析的效率，突破了现有技术基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SIMON密码适用于物联网设备，但其密码分析效率低下，主要原因是差分分布表规模大和高概率差分稀缺。需要改进密码分析效率。

Method: 引入SIMON32密码的差分特性分析，通过部分差分分布表识别高概率差分，揭示了差分特征。

Result: 分析增加了目标轮数，识别了SIMON密码中的高概率差分，超越了现有技术基准。

Conclusion: 对SIMON32密码的差分特性分析为未来效率提升奠定了基础，通过识别高概率差分改进了密码分析方法。

Abstract: SIMON and SPECK were among the first efficient encryption algorithms introduced for resource-constrained applications. SIMON is suitable for Internet of Things (IoT) devices and has rapidly attracted the attention of the research community to understand its structure and analyse its security. To analyse the security of an encryption algorithm, researchers often employ cryptanalysis techniques. However, cryptanalysis is a resource and time-intensive task. To improve cryptanalysis efficiency, state-of-the-art research has proposed implementing heuristic search and sampling methods. Despite recent advances, the cryptanalysis of the SIMON cypher remains inefficient. Contributing factors are the large size of the difference distribution tables utilised in cryptanalysis and the scarcity of differentials with a high transition probability. To address these limitations, we introduce an analysis of differential properties of the SIMON32 cypher, revealing differential characteristics that pave the way for future efficiency enhancements. Our analysis has further increased the number of targeted rounds by identifying high probability differentials within a partial difference distribution table of the SIMON cypher, exceeding existing state-of-the-art benchmarks. The code designed for this work is available at https://github.com/johncook1979/simon32-analysis.

</details>


### [171] [Quantifying Memory Cells Vulnerability for DRAM Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18549)
*Zilong Hu,Hongming Fei,Prosanta Gope,Jack Miskelly,Owen Millwood,Biplab Sikdar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个定量、单元级的电路框架，从物理电荷泄漏和干扰路径直接建模DRAM漏洞，将器件层行为与系统级安全属性关联，用于评估DRAM在安全应用中的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DRAM单元漏洞（强制保留失效、延迟改变、行锤击和行压力）既可能被攻击者利用破坏数据完整性和机密性，也可用于生成随机密钥和设备指纹。攻击者可能利用对单元翻转漏洞的了解来预测数据内容，从而破坏安全系统。需要系统性地评估DRAM在安全应用中的适用性。

Method: 开发一个定量、单元级的电路框架，直接从DRAM的物理电荷泄漏和干扰路径建模其漏洞。通过将器件层行为（电荷泄漏、干扰效应）与系统级安全属性（易失性、完整性、机密性）联系起来，实现系统化评估。

Result: 该框架能够系统评估DRAM的易失性（保留特性）、完整性（干扰引起的修改）和机密性（模式依赖的泄漏）。应用于已知故障模式时，揭示了非均匀和上下文依赖的漏洞模式。

Conclusion: 这项工作为评估DRAM在安全应用中的适用性提供了理论基础和实际评估工具，能够分析DRAM漏洞如何影响安全属性，并为安全系统设计提供指导。

Abstract: Dynamic Random Access Memory (DRAM) is pervasive in computer systems. Cell vulnerabilities caused by unintended phenomena (forced retention failure, latency alteration, rowhammer and rowpress) lead to unintended bit flips in memory. These phenomena have been explored as attacks to violate data integrity and confidentiality during normal operation, but also exploited as a benefit in security systems as a method to generate random secret keys and unique device fingerprints (e.g. Physically Unclonable Functions). In both cases, attackers may wish to exploit knowledge of individual cell flip vulnerability to predict the current/future data contents of a set of cells, which can be utilised to break security systems. In this work, we develop a quantitative, cell-level circuit framework that models DRAM vulnerability directly from its physical charge leakage and disturbance pathways. By linking these device-layer behaviours to system-level security properties, our framework enables systematic evaluation of DRAM with respect to volatility (retention), integrity (disturbance-induced modification), and confidentiality (pattern-dependent leakage). We further demonstrate how the framework can be applied to well-known failure modes, revealing non-uniform and context-dependent vulnerability patterns. This work provides both theoretical foundations and practical evaluation tools for evaluating the suitability of DRAM use within security applications.

</details>


### [172] [A Complexity Hierarchy of Shuffles in Card-Based Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18608)
*Tomoki Ono,Suthee Ruangwises*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了基于纸牌密码学中洗牌操作的复杂度层次结构，证明了不同层次间的分离性，并基于此提出了新的协议复杂度度量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于纸牌的密码协议使用各种类型的洗牌操作，其中一些在实践中易于实现，而另一些则复杂得多。需要对这些洗牌操作进行系统分类，从理论和实践角度理解其实现复杂度，并建立形式化的层次结构。

Method: 将洗牌操作按照实现复杂度分为多个层次，从实践和理论角度论证这一层次结构的合理性，通过证明某些洗牌操作无法仅用低层次操作实现来展示层次间的分离性。

Result: 建立了洗牌操作的复杂度层次结构，证明了不同层次之间的分离结果，表明某些洗牌确实需要更高层次的操作才能实现。基于这一层次结构提出了评估基于纸牌协议的新复杂度度量方法。

Conclusion: 洗牌操作可以根据实现复杂度进行层次化分类，这一分类具有理论和实践意义。提出的层次结构为评估和比较基于纸牌的密码协议提供了新的框架和复杂度度量标准。

Abstract: Card-based cryptography uses physical playing cards to construct protocols for secure multi-party computation. Existing card-based protocols employ various types of shuffles, some of which are easy to implement in practice while others are considerably more complex. In this paper, we classify shuffle operations into several levels according to their implementation complexity. We motivate this hierarchy from both practical and theoretical perspectives, and prove separation results between several levels by showing that certain shuffles cannot be realized using only operations from lower levels. Finally, we propose a new complexity measure for evaluating card-based protocols based on this hierarchy.

</details>


### [173] [MOSAIC: Multi-Objective Slice-Aware Iterative Curation for Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18637)
*Yipu Dou,Wang Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MOSAIC是一个多目标切片感知迭代优化框架，用于在固定监督微调预算下平衡多轮安全对齐、低良性边界查询过拒绝和可验证约束下的指令跟随三个目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前需要在有限监督微调预算下同时平衡三个关键对齐目标：多轮安全对齐、减少对良性边界查询的过拒绝、以及在可验证约束下的指令跟随能力。传统方法难以系统性地在这些相互竞争的目标间进行权衡。

Method: 提出MOSAIC框架，基于统一的L1-L3评估接口构建闭环数据混合搜索系统。将切片级别的失败分析转化为可执行的数据操作，包括数据集级混合比例、桶级权重和焦点标准。

Result: 在固定100万token预算和五轮独立微调下，MOSAIC将内部XGuard评分从2.76提升到4.67，同时保持OrBench在4.41和IFEval在3.65。最终Pareto解在独立攻击、过拒绝和能力测试上比随机静态LoRA基线有更好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 结构化失败诊断可以作为预算化数据构建的实用控制信号，MOSAIC框架能够有效指导在有限监督微调预算下的多目标对齐优化。

Abstract: We study how to allocate a fixed supervised fine-tuning budget when three objectives must be balanced at once: multi-turn safety alignment, low over-refusal on benign boundary queries, and instruction following under verifiable constraints. We propose MOSAIC (Multi-Objective Slice-Aware Iterative Curation for Alignment), a multi-objective framework for closed-loop data mixture search built on a unified L1-L3 evaluation interface. MOSAIC turns slice-level failure profiles into executable data actions, including dataset-level mixture ratios, bucket-level weights, and focus criteria. Under a fixed 1M-token budget and five rounds of independent fine-tuning from the same base model, MOSAIC improves internal XGuard from 2.76 to 4.67 while keeping OrBench at 4.41 and IFEval at 3.65. The final Pareto solution also generalizes better than a random static LoRA baseline on independent attack, over-refusal, and capability tests, suggesting that structured failure diagnosis can serve as a practical control signal for budgeted data construction. Code is available at https://github.com/douyipu/mosaic.

</details>


### [174] [Secure Wi-Fi Ranging Today: Security and Adoption of IEEE 802.11az/bk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18687)
*Nikola Antonijević,Bernhard Etzlinger,Dave Singelée,Bart Preneel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文分析IEEE 802.11az和802.11bk标准中的安全Wi-Fi测距机制，从安全和部署角度评估其实际效果，揭示配置敏感性和硬件实现困难


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着测距和定位在应用中的重要性增加，Wi-Fi标准成为自然选择。IEEE 802.11az引入了"下一代定位"机制来加固不安全的FTM测距方案，802.11bk通过增加带宽接近UWB的厘米级精度。但需要从安全和部署角度评估这些承诺的实际效果

Method: 结合标准分析、仿真和商用/开发硬件测量，从逻辑层和物理层分析安全Wi-Fi测距机制。逻辑层分析部署选择导致的安全问题；物理层研究安全测距波形的可预测性、符号重复的安全影响以及波形设计对频谱掩模合规性的影响

Result: 安全Wi-Fi测距对配置选择高度敏感，在现有硬件上实现非易事。逻辑层显示常见部署可能导致未认证测距、降级攻击和简单拒绝服务攻击；物理层发现波形可预测性、符号重复安全影响等问题。目前商用设备对安全Wi-Fi测距支持有限

Conclusion: 论文提供安全使用FTM的实用指南，并向厂商和标准化机构提出改进建议，以增强安全Wi-Fi测距的鲁棒性和可部署性

Abstract: Ranging and localisation have become critical for many applications and services. The Wi-Fi (IEEE 802.11) standard is a natural candidate for providing these functions across diverse environments, given its widespread deployment. The IEEE 802.11az amendment, finalised in 2023, introduces "Next Generation Positioning" mechanisms to secure and harden the existing insecure Wi-Fi Fine Timing Measurement (FTM) ranging solution. Moreover, the recent IEEE 802.11bk amendment increases the available bandwidth with the goal of approaching the centimetre-level ranging accuracy of ultra-wideband (UWB) systems. This paper examines to what extent these promises hold from a security and deployability perspective. We analyse the core mechanisms of secure Wi-Fi ranging as defined in IEEE 802.11az and IEEE 802.11bk at both the logical and physical layers, combining standards analysis with simulations and measurements on commercial and development hardware. At the logical layer, we show how common deployment choices can result in unauthenticated ranging, downgrade attacks, and simple denial-of-service attacks, making it difficult to securely realise many high-stakes use cases. At the physical layer, we study the predictability of secure ranging waveforms, the security impact of symbol repetition, and how waveform design choices affect compliance with spectral masks under realistic RF behaviour. Our results show that secure Wi-Fi ranging is highly sensitive to configuration choices and is non-trivial to implement on existing hardware. This is also evidenced by the currently limited support for secure Wi-Fi ranging in commodity devices. This paper provides practical guidelines for using secure FTM safely and recommendations to vendors and standardisation bodies to improve its robustness and deployability.

</details>


### [175] [Cross-Ecosystem Vulnerability Analysis for Python Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18693)
*Georgios Alexopoulos,Nikolaos Alexopoulos,Thodoris Sotiropoulos,Charalambos Mitropoulos,Zhendong Su,Dimitris Mitropoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种溯源感知的漏洞分析方法，通过内容哈希和动态分析识别Python包中集成的原生库版本，构建跨生态系统调用图进行漏洞可达性分析，显著减少误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python应用依赖的原生库可能以供应商形式打包在包分发中或安装在主机系统上。当这些库中发现漏洞时，确定哪些Python包受影响需要进行跨生态系统分析。现有漏洞扫描器会因忽略供应商漏洞而产生漏报，或因忽略操作系统发行版的安全补丁而产生误报。

Method: 1) 溯源感知的漏洞分析方法，将供应商库解析到特定操作系统包版本或上游版本；2) 使用基于内容的哈希查询供应商库与历史操作系统包工件数据库；3) 应用库特定的动态分析从上游源码构建的二进制文件中提取版本信息；4) 通过跨依赖边界拼接Python和二进制调用图来构建跨生态系统调用图；5) 对易受攻击函数进行可达性分析。

Result: 在10万个Python包和10个已知的第三方原生依赖CVE上评估，识别出39个直接易受攻击的包（月下载量超过4700万）和312个通过依赖链间接易受攻击的客户端包。与上游版本匹配相比，分析实现了高达97%的误报减少。

Conclusion: 提出的溯源感知漏洞分析方法能够有效识别Python包中供应商原生库的漏洞，通过精确的版本识别和跨生态系统调用图分析，显著提高了漏洞检测的准确性，减少了误报和漏报。

Abstract: Python applications depend on native libraries that may be vendored within package distributions or installed on the host system. When vulnerabilities are discovered in these libraries, determining which Python packages are affected requires cross-ecosystem analysis spanning Python dependency graphs and OS package versions. Current vulnerability scanners produce false negatives by missing vendored vulnerabilities and false positives by ignoring security patches backported by OS distributions.
  We present a provenance-aware vulnerability analysis approach that resolves vendored libraries to specific OS package versions or upstream releases. Our approach queries vendored libraries against a database of historical OS package artifacts using content-based hashing, and applies library-specific dynamic analyses to extract version information from binaries built from upstream source. We then construct cross-ecosystem call graphs by stitching together Python and binary call graphs across dependency boundaries, enabling reachability analysis of vulnerable functions. Evaluating on 100,000 Python packages and 10 known CVEs associated with third-party native dependencies, we identify 39 directly vulnerable packages (47M+ monthly downloads) and 312 indirectly vulnerable client packages affected through dependency chains. Our analysis achieves up to 97% false positive reduction compared to upstream version matching.

</details>


### [176] [Functional Subspace Watermarking for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18793)
*Zikang Ding,Junhao Li,Suling Wu,Junchi Yao,Hongbo Liu,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FSW是一种通过将水印锚定在低维功能子空间来增强大语言模型所有权保护鲁棒性的框架，能抵抗微调、量化等参数扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型水印方法在面临实际模型修改（如微调、量化、知识蒸馏）时，内部特征会发生复杂畸变，导致水印提取不可靠，缺乏对参数级扰动的足够鲁棒性。

Method: 提出功能子空间水印（FSW）：1）通过求解广义特征值问题提取稳定的功能子空间用于水印注入；2）引入自适应谱截断策略平衡鲁棒性与模型效用；3）加入向量一致性约束确保水印注入不影响原始语义性能。

Result: 在多种LLM架构和数据集上的实验表明，FSW在多种模型攻击下实现了优越的检测精度和统计可验证性，其鲁棒性优于现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: FSW通过将所有权信号锚定在低维功能骨干中，有效解决了现有模型水印方法对参数级扰动鲁棒性不足的问题，为LLM所有权保护提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Model watermarking utilizes internal representations to protect the ownership of large language models (LLMs). However, these features inevitably undergo complex distortions during realistic model modifications such as fine-tuning, quantization, or knowledge distillation, making reliable extraction extremely challenging. Despite extensive research on model-side watermarking, existing methods still lack sufficient robustness against parameter-level perturbations. To address this gap, we propose \texttt{\textbf{Functional Subspace Watermarking (FSW)}}, a framework that anchors ownership signals into a low-dimensional functional backbone. Specifically, we first solve a generalized eigenvalue problem to extract a stable functional subspace for watermark injection, while introducing an adaptive spectral truncation strategy to achieve an optimal balance between robustness and model utility. Furthermore, a vector consistency constraint is incorporated to ensure that watermark injection does not compromise the original semantic performance. Extensive experiments across various LLM architectures and datasets demonstrate that our method achieves superior detection accuracy and statistical verifiability under multiple model attacks, maintaining robustness that outperforms existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods.

</details>


### [177] [Agent Control Protocol: Admission Control for Agent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18829)
*Marcelo Fernandez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Agent Control Protocol (ACP) 是一个用于B2B机构环境中自主智能体治理的正式技术规范，作为智能体意图和系统状态变更之间的准入控制层，通过密码学验证确保操作合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自主智能体在B2B机构环境中的治理问题，确保智能体操作在明确的机构控制下进行，同时保持与现有RBAC和零信任架构的兼容性。

Method: 定义密码学身份、基于能力的授权、确定性风险评估、可验证链式委托、传递式撤销和不可变审计等机制，作为RBAC和零信任之上的附加层。

Result: v1.13规范包含36个技术文档、5个符合性等级、Go参考实现、51个签名符合性测试向量、OpenAPI 3.1.0规范，定义了62+可验证要求、12个禁止行为和机构间互操作性机制。

Conclusion: ACP为自主智能体在机构环境中的安全可控操作提供了完整的技术框架，通过密码学准入控制确保治理合规性，同时保持与现有安全架构的兼容性。

Abstract: Agent Control Protocol (ACP) is a formal technical specification for governance of autonomous agents in B2B institutional environments. ACP is the admission control layer between agent intent and system state mutation: before any agent action reaches execution, it must pass a cryptographic admission check that validates identity, capability scope, delegation chain, and policy compliance simultaneously.
  ACP defines the mechanisms of cryptographic identity, capability-based authorization, deterministic risk evaluation, verifiable chained delegation, transitive revocation, and immutable auditing that a system must implement for autonomous agents to operate under explicit institutional control. ACP operates as an additional layer on top of RBAC and Zero Trust, without replacing them.
  The v1.13 specification comprises 36 technical documents organized into five conformance levels (L1-L5). It includes a Go reference implementation of 22 packages covering all L1-L4 capabilities, 51 signed conformance test vectors (Ed25519 + SHA-256), and an OpenAPI 3.1.0 specification for all HTTP endpoints. It defines more than 62 verifiable requirements, 12 prohibited behaviors, and the mechanisms for interoperability between institutions.
  Specification and implementation: https://github.com/chelof100/acp-framework-en

</details>


### [178] [Confidential Databases Without Cryptographic Mappings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18836)
*Wenxuan Huang,Zhanbo Wang,Mingyu Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FEDB是一种新型机密数据库设计，通过将密码学操作移出关键路径，使用无密码映射技术，显著降低性能开销


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密数据库在不可信云环境中保护敏感数据查询，但现有方案因频繁的同步密码学操作导致高性能开销，阻碍了广泛部署

Method: 采用无密码映射技术，在数据库中维护数据无关的标识符，同时在可信域中安全地将这些标识符映射到明文秘密，从而将密码学操作移出关键路径

Result: 在TPC-C和TPC-H等行业标准基准测试中，运行时开销降低了高达78.0倍

Conclusion: FEDB通过范式转变显著降低了机密数据库的性能开销，为更广泛部署提供了可行方案

Abstract: Confidential databases (CDBs) are essential for enabling secure queries over sensitive data in untrusted cloud environments using confidential computing hardware. While adoption is growing, widespread deployment is hindered by high performance overhead from frequent synchronous cryptographic operations, which causes significant computational and memory bottlenecks. We present FEDB, a novel CDB design that removes cryptographic operations from the critical path. FEDB leverages crypto-free mappings, which maintain data-independent identifiers within the database while securely mapping them to plaintext secrets in a trusted domain. This paradigm shift reduces the runtime overhead by up to 78.0 times on industry-standard benchmarks including TPC-C and TPC-H.

</details>


### [179] [Controller Datapath Aware Verification of Masked Hardware Generated via High Level Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18939)
*Nilotpola Sarma,Vaishali Ghanshyam Chaudhuri,Chandan Karfa*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出MaskedHLSVerif工具流，用于验证HLS生成的掩码硬件设计的侧信道安全性，避免现有工具因资源共享数据路径导致的误报


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HLS工具可自动从已验证的掩码软件生成掩码RTL硬件，但HLS优化可能影响侧信道安全性。现有硬件掩码验证工具在验证HLS生成的控制器-数据路径架构时可能产生误报

Method: 提出MaskedHLSVerif工具流，对HLS生成的控制器-数据路径RTL进行状态级形式化验证，避免因资源共享数据路径导致的误报

Result: 工具流正确验证了级联掩码模块和PRESENT S-box掩码实现等标准密码学基准，而现有工具如REBECCA报告误报。该工具流还能检测HLS优化引入的掩码缺陷

Conclusion: MaskedHLSVerif为HLS生成的掩码硬件提供有效的侧信道安全性验证方法，解决了现有工具在验证资源共享数据路径架构时的误报问题

Abstract: Masking is a countermeasure against Power Side Channel Attacks (PSCAs) in both software and hardware implementations of cryptographic algorithms. Compared to software masking, implementing masked hardware is time consuming and error prone. Recent approaches, therefore, rely on High Level Synthesis (HLS) tools to automatically generate masked Register Transfer Level (RTL) hardware from verified masked software, significantly reducing design effort. Since HLS was never developed for security, HLS optimizations may impact PSCA security of the generated RTL. As a result, verifying the PSCA security of HLS generated masked RTL is crucial. Existing hardware masking verification tools can verify masked hardware, but may produce false positives when applied to HLS generated designs with controller datapath architectures obtained due to resource-shared datapath obtained via HLS. This work proposes a hardware masking verification strategy for HLS generated masked hardware. Our toolflow MaskedHLSVerif, performs state-wise formal verification of controller datapath RTL obtained via HLS, thereby avoiding false positives caused by resource-shared datapaths. Our tool flow correctly verifies standard cryptographic benchmarks, consisting of cascaded masked gadgets and the PRESENT S-box masked with gadgets, where existing tools like REBECCA reports false positives. The proposed tool-flow is able to detect masking flaws induced by HLS-optimizations as well.

</details>


### [180] [FedTrident: Resilient Road Condition Classification Against Poisoning Attacks in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19101)
*Sheng Liu,Panos Papadimitratos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FedTrident是一个针对联邦学习路况分类中目标标签翻转攻击的防御框架，通过神经元分析检测恶意模型、自适应客户端评分排除恶意客户端、以及机器遗忘修复全局模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在智能交通系统路况分类中面临目标标签翻转攻击威胁，现有防御方法在多种攻击场景下无法维持接近无攻击水平的模型性能，主要因为：1)未针对TLFAs定制本地模型检测；2)未基于历史行为排除恶意客户端；3)排除后未修复已损坏的全局模型。

Method: FedTrident包含三个核心组件：1)基于神经元分析的本地模型异常检测（包括攻击目标识别、关键特征提取、GMM聚类过滤）；2)根据每轮FL检测结果进行自适应客户端评分以排除恶意客户端；3)使用机器遗忘技术在排除恶意客户端后修复已损坏的全局模型。

Result: 在多种FL-RCC模型、任务和配置下的广泛评估表明，FedTrident能有效抵御TLFAs，在无攻击场景下达到可比性能，在两个最关键指标上分别优于8个基线防御方法9.49%和4.47%。对恶意客户端率、数据异构性、复杂多任务和动态攻击具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FedTrident填补了联邦学习路况分类中针对目标标签翻转攻击的防御研究空白，通过集成检测、排除和修复机制，实现了接近无攻击水平的模型性能，为智能交通系统安全提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: FL has emerged as a transformative paradigm for ITS, notably camera-based Road Condition Classification (RCC). However, by enabling collaboration, FL-based RCC exposes the system to adversarial participants launching Targeted Label-Flipping Attacks (TLFAs). Malicious clients (vehicles) can relabel their local training data (e.g., from an actual uneven road to a wrong smooth road), consequently compromising global model predictions and jeopardizing transportation safety. Existing countermeasures against such poisoning attacks fail to maintain resilient model performance near the necessary attack-free levels in various attack scenarios due to: 1) not tailoring poisoned local model detection to TLFAs, 2) not excluding malicious vehicular clients based on historical behavior, and 3) not remedying the already-corrupted global model after exclusion. To close this research gap, we propose FedTrident, which introduces: 1) neuron-wise analysis for local model misbehavior detection (notably including attack goal identification, critical feature extraction, and GMM-based model clustering and filtering); 2) adaptive client rating for client exclusion according to the local model detection results in each FL round; and 3) machine unlearning for corrupted global model remediation once malicious clients are excluded during FL. Extensive evaluation across diverse FL-RCC models, tasks, and configurations demonstrates that FedTrident can effectively thwart TLFAs, achieving performance comparable to that in attack-free scenarios and outperforming eight baseline countermeasures by 9.49% and 4.47% for the two most critical metrics. Moreover, FedTrident is resilient to various malicious client rates, data heterogeneity levels, complicated multi-task, and dynamic attacks.

</details>


### [181] [Performance Testing of ChaCha20-Poly1305 for Internet of Things and Industrial Control System devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19150)
*Kristján Orri Ragnarsson,Jacky Mallett*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究测量了在低成本边缘设备上为工业控制系统协议添加加密的时间成本，发现即使使用ChaCha20-Poly1305加密，其开销也远低于协议延迟要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业控制系统和物联网设备常使用未加密或未认证的协议通信，这是历史遗留问题。随着小型设备处理能力指数级增长和高效加密算法出现，终端设备加密变得可行，但Modbus和IEC61850等协议对延迟和方差有严格要求，加密应用仍不广泛。

Method: 研究测量了在低成本边缘设备（Raspberry PI 4和Intel N95 Mini PC）上为通信周期添加ChaCha20-Poly1305加密的时间成本，考虑了动态频率缩放等现代微处理器特性对性能测量的影响。

Result: 在最坏情况下，加密周期仅占用GOOSE协议延迟要求的7.1%，对IEC-60834-1协议则低于3%，远低于这些协议的指定延迟要求，证明加密在工业控制系统协议中的可行性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，即使在延迟要求严格的工业控制系统协议中，现代低成本边缘设备也能在满足延迟要求的前提下实现通信加密，解决了历史遗留的安全问题。

Abstract: Industrial Control Systems (ICS), and many simple Internet of Things (IoT) devices, commonly communicate using unencrypted or unauthenticated protocols. For ICS this is an historical carryover since the introduction of these systems predated practical lightweight cryptography. As the processing power of small devices has grown exponentially at the same time as new, more efficient encryption algorithms have become available, end device encryption of communication protocols is becoming much more practical, but is still not widely used with ICS protocols such as Modbus and IEC61850 (GOOSE) which have tight requirements for both latency and variance. Newer micro-processors can also present challenges both to measurement and use, since features such as dynamic frequency scaling can significantly impact performance measurements. In this paper, we measured the time cost of adding encryption into the communication cycle of low-cost edge devices using ChaCha20-Poly1305, and show that in the worst case the encryption cycle took less than 7.1 percent of the latency requirements of Goose, and less than 3% for IEC-60834-1 on Raspberry PI 4, and an Intel N95 Mini PC, which is well within the specified latency requirements for these protocols.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Starting Off on the Wrong Foot: Pitfalls in Data Preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18190)
*Jiayi Guo,Panyi Dong,Zhiyu Quan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出针对保险损失数据的数据准备框架，结合支持点数据分割和Chatterjee相关系数特征筛选，提升模型稳健性和计算效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机训练测试划分在处理高度不平衡的保险损失数据时产生不可靠结果，需要更统计严谨的数据准备方法来确保下游建模的有效性

Method: 提出统一数据准备框架：1) 使用支持点进行代表性数据分割确保分布一致性；2) 使用Chatterjee相关系数进行非参数特征筛选；3) 集成缺失数据处理；4) 嵌入自定义InsurAutoML管道

Result: 在模拟数据集和学术文献常用数据集上的评估表明，该方法显著增强模型稳健性和可解释性，同时大幅减少计算资源需求

Conclusion: 统计严谨的数据准备方法为高风险保险应用提供了关键的方法学升级，是实现可靠结果的重要基础

Abstract: When working with real-world insurance data, practitioners often encounter challenges during the data preparation stage that can undermine the statistical validity and reliability of downstream modeling. This study illustrates that conventional data preparation procedures such as random train-test partitioning, often yield unreliable and unstable results when confronted with highly imbalanced insurance loss data. To mitigate these limitations, we propose a novel data preparation framework leveraging two recent statistical advancements: support points for representative data splitting to ensure distributional consistency across partitions, and the Chatterjee correlation coefficient for initial, non-parametric feature screening to capture feature relevance and dependence structure. We further integrate these theoretical advances into a unified, efficient framework that also incorporates missing-data handling, and embed this framework within our custom InsurAutoML pipeline. The performance of the proposed approach is evaluated using both simulated datasets and datasets often cited in the academic literature. Our findings definitively demonstrate that incorporating statistically rigorous data preparation methods not only significantly enhances model robustness and interpretability but also substantially reduces computational resource requirements across diverse insurance loss modeling tasks. This work provides a crucial methodological upgrade for achieving reliable results in high stakes insurance applications.

</details>


### [183] [Multi-Domain Causal Empirical Bayes Under Linear Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18404)
*Bohan Wu,Julius von Kügelgen,David M. Blei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于经验贝叶斯(EB)的因果表示学习方法，通过多领域数据利用共享因果模型中的干预结构来改进因果变量估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果表示学习(CRL)旨在从高维观测中学习低维因果潜变量。虽然CRL的可识别性已得到广泛研究，但其估计方法较少被探索。本文探索使用经验贝叶斯(EB)来估计因果表示，特别是在多领域数据设置中，其中领域差异由共享底层因果模型中的干预建模。

Method: 提出一种EB f-建模算法，利用领域内和跨领域的不变结构改进因果变量学习。考虑线性测量模型和来自共享无环SCM的干预先验。当图和干预目标已知时，开发基于因果结构得分匹配的EM风格算法。同时讨论了EB g-建模在现有CRL方法中的背景。

Result: 在合成数据实验中，提出的方法比其他CRL方法实现了更准确的估计。

Conclusion: 经验贝叶斯方法在多领域因果表示学习中具有优势，能够通过利用干预结构提高因果变量估计的准确性。

Abstract: Causal representation learning (CRL) aims to learn low-dimensional causal latent variables from high-dimensional observations. While identifiability has been extensively studied for CRL, estimation has been less explored. In this paper, we explore the use of empirical Bayes (EB) to estimate causal representations. In particular, we consider the problem of learning from data from multiple domains, where differences between domains are modeled by interventions in a shared underlying causal model. Multi-domain CRL naturally poses a simultaneous inference problem that EB is designed to tackle. Here, we propose an EB $f$-modeling algorithm that improves the quality of learned causal variables by exploiting invariant structure within and across domains. Specifically, we consider a linear measurement model and interventional priors arising from a shared acyclic SCM. When the graph and intervention targets are known, we develop an EM-style algorithm based on causally structured score matching. We further discuss EB $\rmg$-modeling in the context of existing CRL approaches. In experiments on synthetic data, our proposed method achieves more accurate estimation than other methods for CRL.

</details>


### [184] [Statistical Testing Framework for Clustering Pipelines by Selective Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18413)
*Yugo Miyata,Tomohiro Shiraishi,Shunichi Nishino,Ichiro Takeuchi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出基于选择性推断的统计测试框架，用于评估聚类分析流水线结果的统计可靠性，控制I类错误率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据流水线通过多个数据依赖步骤产生分析结果，但缺乏量化这些结果统计可靠性的方法，特别是对于包含异常检测、特征选择、聚类等步骤的复杂聚类流水线

Method: 基于选择性推断的统计测试框架，为预定义组件组成的聚类流水线系统构建有效统计测试，通过选择性推断方法处理数据依赖的多步骤过程

Result: 理论证明该方法能在任意名义水平控制I类错误率，在合成和真实数据集上的实验验证了其有效性和正确性

Conclusion: 提出的选择性推断框架为评估聚类流水线结果的统计显著性提供了系统方法，填补了量化复杂数据分析流程可靠性的空白
